- **psicoterapia-mx**: Plugin para psicólogos/psicoterapeutas en práctica privada en México. Cubre agenda de sesiones, expediente psicológico confidencial (con cifrado AES-256 extra estricto, datos sensibles LGPDPPSO), consentimiento informado terapéutico, cobranza recurrente con plantillas empáticas, dashboard mensual. Score research: 7.8/10. ~50k psicólogos clínicos en MX. ([elimorals/Skills_MX](https://github.com/elimorals/Skills_MX/tree/HEAD/psicoterapia-mx) · ★29 · 5 skills, 4 commands)

- **pskoett-ai-skills**: A self-improving agent skill pipeline that prevents common AI coding agent failures. Includes 6 skills (plan-interview, intent-framed-agent, context-surfing, simplify-and-harden, self-improvement, agent-teams) and 5 subagents (simplify-auditor, harden-auditor, spec-auditor, context-monitor, self-improvement-logger). The pipeline catches scope drift, context degradation, security gaps, and repeated mistakes — each skill targets a specific failure mode and hands off cleanly to the next. ([anthropics/claude-plugins-community](https://github.com/anthropics/claude-plugins-community/tree/HEAD/plugin) · ★4.9k)
- **pskoett-ai-skills**: Two-loop skill pipeline for Claude Code. Inner loop (plan-interview, intent-framed-agent, session-context, context-surfing, verify-gate, self-healing, simplify-and-harden) catches failures in-session — including active runtime recovery via self-healing when a command, test, build, helper, env, or external service fails mid-task. Outer loop (learning-aggregator, self-improvement, eval-creator, pre-flight-check) captures recurring patterns, promotes them to CLAUDE.md as permanent rules with human approval, and turns failures into regression tests. The harness gets stronger with every cycle. ([pskoett/pskoett-ai-skills](https://github.com/pskoett/pskoett-ai-skills/tree/HEAD/plugin) · ★339 · 17 skills, 1 hook file)
